<div id="indexh1">
<h1><span style="color: #000; font-weight: 600;">About</span> Bargain Hardware</h1>
</div>
<div id="customcms">
<div style="padding: 0px 20px 20px 15px;">
<p>Bargain Hardware is Europe’s leading supplier of used and refurbished custom configured business and data centre IT hardware, including <a href="https://bargainhardware.co.uk/refurbished-servers">refurbished servers</a>, workstations, desktop PCs, laptops and components. <br/><br/>For over 20 years we have been providing our customers with a low cost alternative to new hardware but with the freedom to create a completely custom specification. We hold one of Europe’s largest stocks of HP ProLiant, Dell PowerEdge, IBM and Lenovo servers along with Desktop PCs and Workstations including <a href="https://bargainhardware.co.uk/refurbished-desktop-pcs/hp">HP EliteDesk</a> and Dell Precision. All build-to-order systems undergo rigorous diagnostic testing to ensure ‘as new’ functionality and are supplied with a minimum 1 year return-to-base warranty. <a href="javascript:toggle();" id="displayText"><strong>Read More</strong></a></p>
<div id="toggleText" style="display: none;">
<p>With a product catalogue of over 10,000 items we provide huge savings on all components from <a href="https://bargainhardware.co.uk/components/processors-cpu/intel-xeon">Intel Xeon CPUs </a>to <a href="https://bargainhardware.co.uk/components/enterprise-hdds-sdds-storage">SATA &amp; SAS server hard drives or SSDs</a>. Our thorough testing and disassembly process makes reliable, cheap spare parts available for a broad range of systems, from legacy <a href="https://bargainhardware.co.uk/refurbished-desktop-pcs/dell">Dell OptiPlex PCs </a>to the latest and greatest <a href="https://bargainhardware.co.uk/refurbished-workstations/hp">HP Z-series workstations</a>.</p>
<p>We only advertise inventory held in our own warehouse, allowing for express UPS shipping to reach all major European cities within 24 hours for urgent parts and server options including CPU kits, motherboards and replacement power supplies. Custom made packaging solutions including RAM and CPU trays ensure that your delivery is professionally packaged and arrives ready for installation.</p>
<p>Whether you're looking to build a <a href="https://bargainhardware.co.uk/refurbished-servers/home-lab-servers">VMware homelab server</a> for vSphere training or a <a href="https://bargainhardware.co.uk/refurbished-servers/dell-1u">Dell PowerEdge</a> stack for your private cloud hosting data center, refurbished IT equipment from Bargain Hardware allows your budget to stretch further without comprising on reliability or performance. From <a href="https://bargainhardware.co.uk/refurbished-servers/data-center-specials">cheap cloud servers</a> for data center deployments and render farm expansions to high-end AVID workstations and server memory upgrades, we stock the parts and components to deliver your configure-to-order systems on-time, on-budget and backed by expert support.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
